### [1] [Personalized Recommendation Tool Learning via Autonomous Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19739)
*Mingdai Yang,Zhiwei Liu,Weizhi Zhang,Yibo Wang,Hao Peng,Philip Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PRTA框架，通过LLM作为中央规划器与多个传统推荐模型（作为工具）协作，利用反思机制实现个性化工具选择，在三个数据集上超越传统推荐和LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐代理存在幻觉和上下文长度限制问题，不适合全排序推荐任务；作者希望通过架构设计而非修改LLM本身来规避这些限制。

Method: 提出PRTA框架：LLM作为中央规划器负责高层推理和个性化工具选择，多个传统推荐模型作为工具执行全排序评分；设计反思机制使代理能够基于用户画像和候选排序列表评估并比较各工具对每个用户的适用性。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，PRTA在全排序推荐性能上优于传统推荐方法和LLM基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过架构设计（LLM规划+传统模型执行）而非修改LLM本身，可以有效解决LLM在推荐任务中的幻觉和上下文长度问题，提升全排序推荐性能。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have recently gained traction in recommender systems due to their strong reasoning capabilities and extensive world knowledge, previous LLM-based agents suffer from hallucination and context-length limitations, and thus are not suitable for full-ranking recommendation tasks. To circumvent these limitations through architectural design rather than modifying the LLM itself, we propose an agent-based recommendation framework, memory-based $\textbf{P}$ersonalized $\textbf{R}$ecommendation $\textbf{T}$ool learning via autonomous language $\textbf{A}$gents (PRTA), in which an LLM acts as a central planner interacting with multiple recommendation models as tools. The LLM-based agent is responsible for high-level reasoning and personalized tool selection, while traditional recommendation models perform full-ranking scoring, leveraging their scalability in modeling behavioral patterns. To support personalized tool selection, we design reflection mechanisms that enable the agent to evaluate and compare tools for each user based on user profiles and candidate ranked lists. Extensive experiments across three public datasets demonstrate the superiority of \modelname over traditional recommendation and LLM-based baselines in improving full-ranking recommendation performance.

</details>


### [2] [CIR at iKAT SCAI 2026: Exploring Clarification Need Prediction in Agentic Conversational Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19801)
*Nolwenn Bernard,Jüri Keller,Philipp Schaer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了科隆信息检索组在iKAT SCAI 2026共享任务中的参与情况，使用了一个基于智能体（Agent）的对话搜索系统，该系统集成了查询重写、检索与重排序、答案生成以及澄清需求预测和澄清问题生成等工具，并实验了两种不同的神经澄清需求预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与iKAT SCAI 2026共享任务，探索在对话搜索系统中通过智能体架构和澄清需求预测来提升信息检索性能。

Method: 采用智能体对话搜索系统，集成查询重写、检索与重排序、答案生成、澄清需求预测及澄清问题生成等工具，并对比两种神经澄清需求预测模型。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果，仅提及实验了两种不同的神经澄清需求预测模型。

Conclusion: 未在摘要中明确给出最终结论。

Abstract: This paper presents the participation of the Cologne Information Retrieval group in the iKAT SCAI 2026 shared task. We use an agentic conversational search system, equipped with tools for query rewriting, retrieval and reranking, answer generation, and clarification need prediction and clarification question generation. We experiment with two different neural clarification need prediction models.

</details>


### [3] [Zero-Observation User Reactivation with Gap-Driven Dimensional Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19802)
*Jiandong Ding,Tianying Liu,Fuyuan Liu,Huijie Qin,Tiandeng Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DeltaGate，一种轻量级输出层插件，用于解决序列推荐中的"零观测重激活"问题（用户长时间无交互后再次出现）。该插件保持骨干网络冻结，通过基于间隔时间和个性化表示的条件门控机制，在个性化历史与全局先验之间进行维度级路由。在三个Amazon数据集上，DeltaGate显著提升了长间隔下的推荐性能（如>365天的Video Games数据集上Hit@10从0.031提升至0.047），仅增加2-4%的训练参数，且不改变骨干网络嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐模型通常假设用户行为是连续观测的，但实际中用户可能长时间（数月或数年）无任何交互，导致模型无法捕捉到任何行为信号。这种现象被定义为"零观测重激活"问题，现有SR模型在此场景下性能严重下降（Hit@10随间隔时间单调递减，超过一年时达到最低水平）。提出一个能处理长间隔重激活的轻量级方法。

Method: DeltaGate是一个轻量级输出层插件，核心思想是在时间间隔Δt条件下，对每个表示维度在个性化历史表示与全局先验（零初始化的可学习向量）之间进行门控路由。门控的决策同时依赖于Δt和个性化表示。该方法保持骨干网络完全冻结，仅添加少量可训练参数（约66K，占骨干的2-4%）。同时设计了"Gap-Synthesize Protocol"来构造不同间隔的实验数据，并采用控制变量诊断实验隔离Δt对门控的影响。

Result: 在三个Amazon数据集上，Hit@10随用户间隔时间单调递减，超过一年时达到最低。DeltaGate显著提升了长间隔性能：>365天的Video Games数据集上，DG-SASRec达0.047 vs SASRec的0.031；DG-BERT4Rec达0.046 vs BERT4Rec的0.025。仅66K可训练参数（2-4%开销）。端到端重训练可获得更高绝对精度，但会改变骨干嵌入；而冻结插件的方案保持骨干零漂移、参数减少约40倍，并保留了可观察的维度级路由行为。

Conclusion: DeltaGate有效解决了序列推荐中的零观测重激活问题，通过一个轻量级、冻结骨干的插件方式，在保持原有模型能力不变的前提下，显著提升长间隔用户的推荐效果。该方法参数高效、即插即用、保持骨干零漂移，且门控行为可解释。代码已开源。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation (SR) models capture continuously observed behavior, but a returning user may have no interactions for months or years. We define this setting as Zero-Observation Reactivation: the user has a pre-gap history, while the platform observes no behavioral signals during a macro-gap Delta t. Under a chronologically aligned Gap-Synthesize Protocol on three Amazon datasets (Video Games, CDs & Vinyl, and Movies & TV), Hit@10 decreases monotonically across the evaluated gap buckets and reaches its lowest level beyond one year. The pattern appears across recurrent, unidirectional, and bidirectional SR backbones.
  We propose DeltaGate, a lightweight output-layer plugin that keeps the backbone frozen and routes each representation dimension between the personalized history and a learned, zero-initialized global prior. The gate is conditioned jointly on Delta t and the personalized representation. In a controlled diagnostic, we hold the personalized representation fixed and vary Delta t to isolate the trained gate's response to the gap input. In the >365d Video Games bucket, DG-SASRec reaches 0.047 Hit@10 versus 0.031 for SASRec, while DG-BERT4Rec reaches 0.046 versus 0.025 for BERT4Rec, with 66K trainable parameters (2--4% overhead). End-to-end retraining attains higher absolute accuracy but changes the backbone embeddings; the frozen plugin preserves zero backbone drift, uses about 40x fewer trainable parameters, and retains observable dimension-wise routing. The source code is available at https://github.com/jdding/DeltaGate.

</details>


### [4] [Using Hierarchical Controlled Vocabularies to Understand CLIP Retrieval Failures in Historical Photo Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19836)
*Ratan Sebastian,Anett Hoppe,Christoph Rippe,Ralph Ewerth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了GLAM机构中受控词汇表（如AAT）的结构属性（根面类型和层次深度）如何影响CLIP模型的图像检索性能。发现视觉一致性和文本-图像对齐几乎不相关，且共同区分不同的失败模式；根面类型显著区分视觉一致性类别；微调对层次较浅的术语提升更大，主要改善文本-图像对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究虽知概念抽象度和频率会影响CLIP检索性能，但未从GLAM专业人员已使用的词汇表（如AAT）的结构属性角度解释这种差异。

Method: 使用三个历史摄影集（标注有AAT术语），分析视觉一致性（术语照片在CLIP嵌入空间中的聚类程度）、文本-图像对齐（标签与聚类中心的距离）以及标准检索指标，并考察根面类型和层次深度两个结构属性的影响，同时对比微调前后的效果。

Result: 1) 视觉一致性与文本-图像对齐几乎不相关，两者共同区分不同失败模式；聚类紧密但标签远离的术语检索效果最差。2) 检索指标与结构属性无显著相关，但根面类型能显著区分视觉一致性类别。3) 微调整体提升检索性能，但对层次较浅的术语提升更大，主要改善文本-图像对齐，且超越概念频率的解释力。

Conclusion: AAT的结构属性（尤其是根面类型和层次深度）有助于解释CLIP检索的失败模式与微调效果，为GLAM机构优化图像检索提供了基于现有词汇结构的新视角。

Abstract: GLAM institutions (Galleries, Libraries, Archives, and Museums) organise image access using controlled vocabularies such as the Art and Architecture Thesaurus (AAT). For content-based image retrieval in these settings, vision-language models like CLIP are increasingly used, but their performance varies. This variation is known to relate to measures like concept abstraction and concept frequency. However, no prior work explains this variation in terms of the structural properties of vocabularies like the AAT that GLAM professionals already use. The AAT groups concepts into broad facets (Objects, Activities, Agents, etc.) and arranges terms hierarchically within them. In this paper, we ask whether two structural properties (root facet type and hierarchy depth) explain where CLIP retrieval succeeds and fails, and where fine-tuning helps. Across three historical photographic collections annotated with AAT terms, we examine visual coherence (whether a term's photographs cluster in CLIP's embedding space), text-image alignment (whether its label is near that cluster), and standard retrieval measures, which conflate the two. We find that visual coherence and text-image alignment are nearly uncorrelated across terms and jointly separate distinct failure modes. Terms whose photographs cluster tightly but whose label is distant from the cluster retrieve poorly in every collection, in two of three collections even worse than terms that fail on both metrics. We also show that while retrieval metrics do not correlate significantly with either structural property, root facet type does significantly separate categories with varying visual coherence. Finally, we find that fine-tuning improves retrieval overall, but its gains favour shallower terms in the hierarchy, where text-image alignment improves most, beyond what concept frequency explains.

</details>


### [5] [UniRank: Benchmarking Ranking Models for Unified Sequential Modeling and Feature Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19987)
*Honghao Li,Xianquan Wang,Zibin Zhang,Yi Zhang,Kangyi Lin,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UniRank，一个用于统一序列建模与特征交互的排序模型开源基准。该基准采用时序逐点自回归监督，标准化多种反馈任务的评估，并提供PyTorch工具包（含分布式训练、算子优化、混合精度训练、注意力优化等效率技术）。在5个大规模公开数据集（短视频、广告、电商）上对15个代表性模型进行评测，最大数据集超7亿样本，最长行为序列超10^5。UniRank为统一排序模型的可复现比较、有限资源下的缩放律研究提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排序模型在序列建模与特征交互统一方面虽有进展，但多依赖私有数据、封闭实现和工业级基础设施，导致可复现比较受限，阻碍了缩放律、长序列建模和多任务排序的学术研究。

Method: 提出UniRank开源基准，包含：时序逐点自回归监督方式、多反馈任务标准化评估流程、PyTorch工具包（集成DDP训练、算子优化、混合精度训练、注意力优化等效率优化技术），并在5个大规模公开数据集上对15个统一排序模型进行基准测试。

Result: 在5个大规模公开数据集（短视频、广告、电商）上评测了15个代表性模型，最大数据集超7亿实例，最长行为序列超10^5交互。UniRank提供了可复现的比较基础，支持在有限计算资源下研究缩放律，缩小了学术与工业排序研究之间的差距。

Conclusion: UniRank通过可复现的实验、面向生产的评估和可获取的实现，为研究者、从业者和初学者提供了统一的排序模型基准平台，有助于推动该领域的开放研究。

Abstract: Ranking is a core stage in online advertising and recommender systems. Modern ranking models increasingly unify sequential modeling and feature interaction, yet many advances rely on proprietary data, closed implementations, and large-scale industrial infrastructure. This setting limits reproducible comparison and hinders academic study of scaling laws, long-sequence modeling, and multi-task ranking. To address these limitations, this paper proposes UniRank, an open benchmark for ranking models that unify sequential modeling and feature interaction. UniRank uses chronological pointwise autoregressive supervision, standardizes evaluation across feedback tasks, and provides a PyTorch toolkit with Distributed Data Parallel training, operator optimization, mixed-precision training, attention optimization, and other efficiency techniques that reduce hardware requirements. We benchmark 15 representative unified ranking models on five large-scale public datasets from short-video, advertising, and e-commerce platforms, with the largest dataset containing over 700 million instances and the longest behavior sequence exceeding 10^5 interactions. UniRank provides a reproducible basis for comparing unified ranking models, studying scaling laws under limited compute, and narrowing the gap between academic and industrial ranking research. We believe UniRank benefits researchers, practitioners, and beginners through reproducible experiments, production-oriented evaluation, and accessible implementations. Code and data are available at https://github.com/salmon1802/UniRank.

</details>

### [6] [Understanding Developer Pain Points in Federated Learning: Insights from Stack Overflow and GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19621)
*Sahand Saed,Khairul Alam,Banani Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对495个Stack Overflow帖子和92个FL相关项目的9,116个GitHub议题及拉取请求进行实证分析，识别出联邦学习开发者面临的九大Stack Overflow主题和十三大GitHub主题，发现环境配置与依赖兼容性、API变更与迁移、非独立同分布数据下的训练不稳定、评估与指标正确性、隐私保护机制集成是主要难点，其中“如何”类问题占主导，部分主题如TFF安装与SecureBoost问题具有高未解决率和长解决时间，研究为FL框架设计者、文档作者和教育者提供了可操作的改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习(FL)系统的构建和运维因分布式执行、框架快速演进以及隐私治理要求而面临诸多困难，但缺乏对FL开发者实际挑战的系统性实证研究。本研究旨在通过跨平台(Stack Overflow和GitHub)的大规模数据分析，全面刻画FL开发者的困难模式。

Method: 独立分析495个Stack Overflow帖子(经171,291条初始数据筛选)和来自92个FL项目的9,116个GitHub议题及拉取请求；采用BERTopic主题建模提取主题，利用未解决率、中位解决时间等难度指标量化问题严重程度；并按问题意图(如"How"、"Why"、"What"等)对帖子进行分类。

Result: 识别出Stack Overflow上的9个主导主题和GitHub上的13个主题；持续困难集中在环境设置与依赖兼容性、API断变与迁移、非IID数据下的训练不稳定、评估与指标正确性、隐私保护机制集成等方面；"How"类型问题占主导，反映对程序性指导的强烈需求；TFF安装与环境兼容、联邦特征工程与SecureBoost问题表现出高未解决率和长解决时间。

Conclusion: 联邦学习开发者面临的主要挑战具有可分类的特征模式，且不同平台反映的问题侧重点不同。研究为FL框架设计者、文档作者和教育者提供了改善工具、文档和调试支持的具体方向，并提出了一种可扩展的持续监控开发者痛点的方法论。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without centralizing raw data, but building and operating FL systems remains difficult due to distributed execution, rapidly evolving frameworks, and privacy and governance requirements. In this paper, we present an empirical study of FL developer challenges by independently analyzing 495 Stack Overflow posts and 9,116 GitHub issues and pull requests from 92 FL-related projects. Using BERTopic-based topic modeling and difficulty indicators such as unresolved rates and median resolution time, we characterize recurring problem areas and compare how they manifest across the two support platforms, Stack Overflow and GitHub. Our analysis surfaces nine dominant Stack Overflow topics and thirteen GitHub topics, with persistent difficulties concentrated in environment setup and dependency compatibility, API breakages and migration, training instability under non-IID data, evaluation and metric correctness, and the integration of privacy-preserving mechanisms. We also categorize posts by question intent to understand the kinds of help developers seek; this intent analysis shows that "How"-type questions dominate, reflecting strong demand for procedural guidance. Several topics, such as "TFF Installation and Environment Compatibility" and "Federated Feature Engineering and SecureBoost Issues," exhibit high unresolved rates and long resolution times, suggesting shortcomings in tooling, documentation, and debugging support. Based on these findings, we provide actionable implications for FL framework designers, documentation authors, and educators. Although our results are constrained to public discussions and a subset of widely discussed frameworks, the study offers a scalable method for continuously monitoring developer pain points and improving the usability, reliability, and deployability of FL systems.

</details>


### [7] [PerfAgent: Profiler-Guided Iterative Refinement for Repository-Level Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19653)
*Ryan Deng,Yuanzhe Liu,Bastian Lipka,Yao Ma,Xuhao Chen,Tim Kaler,Jatin Ganhotra*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PerfAgent是一个基于性能剖析器和验证器协同工作的工作流，它能显著提升LLM代理在仓库级代码优化任务中的表现，在GSO和SWE-fficiency-Lite基准上分别将专家级补丁率从19.6%提升至39.2%、从26%提升至74%，超越了OpenHands with GPT-5.1和oracle best-of-five基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在正确性导向的仓库级任务（如SWE-Bench问题修复）上表现良好，但在仓库级代码优化任务中仍存在不足：难以发现隐藏在抽象层和原生扩展背后的性能瓶颈，优化往往停留在表层，且缺乏充分的测试验证，可能导致静默破坏边缘情况。

Method: PerfAgent采用性能剖析器引导（profiler-guided）和验证器在环（verifier-in-the-loop）的工作流。它利用性能剖析器识别真实热点，通过验证器确保补丁行为保持正确，并基于剖析证据而非仅凭运行时间来决定下一步优化方向，从而为现成的编码代理提供所需的反馈循环。

Result: 在GSO基准上，专家级匹配补丁率从19.6%提升至39.2%；在SWE-fficiency-Lite基准上，从26%提升至74%。同时，PerfAgent以更低成本超越了oracle best-of-five基线，表明性能提升来自更好的反馈机制而非额外的测试时采样。

Conclusion: PerfAgent通过引入性能剖析和验证反馈，有效弥补了LLM代理在仓库级代码优化上的不足，证明基于剖析证据的迭代优化策略比简单增加采样次数更高效且成本更低。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now perform well on correctness-oriented repository-level tasks, including SWE-Bench issue resolution and feature implementation in real codebases. However, they still struggle with repository-level code optimization, which requires preserving behavior while improving runtime performance. Passing tests is not enough in this setting; a patch must preserve behavior, implement code optimization, and approach expert speedups. Current agents often miss bottlenecks hidden behind abstraction layers and native extensions, stop after shallow speedups, or insufficiently test the code patches that thus may silently break edge cases. We present PerfAgent, a profiler-guided, verifier-in-the-loop workflow that gives an off-the-shelf coding agent the feedback needed to find real hotspots, improve beyond the first passing patch, and use profiler evidence rather than timing alone to decide what to optimize next. On two challenging optimization benchmarks, GSO and SWE-fficiency-Lite, PerfAgent more than doubles the rate of expert-matching patches over OpenHands with GPT-5.1, improving from 19.6% to 39.2% on GSO and from 26% to 74% on SWE-fficiency-Lite. It also surpasses an oracle best-of-five baseline at substantially lower cost, showing that the gains come from better feedback rather than additional test-time sampling.

</details>


### [8] [Context Matters: Improving the Practical Reliability of LLM-Based Unit Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19682)
*Junjie Chen,Ziqi Wang,Lin Yang,Chen Yang,Xiao Chu,Jianyi Zhou,Guangtai Liang,Qianxiang Wang,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CATGen，一种上下文感知的工作流，通过显式处理项目级依赖、稳定测试类骨架和轻量级静态分析替代迭代LLM修复，显著提升LLM生成单元测试的编译成功率和结构覆盖率，并降低生成时间和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业部署中发现LLM生成的单元测试在实际项目（复杂框架、跨文件依赖）中频繁编译失败、需要昂贵的人工修复或覆盖改进不稳定，存在研究成果与实用性的持续差距。

Method: CATGen采用多阶段设计，结合结构化上下文检索、确定性测试骨架构建和基于程序分析的后处理，而非依赖LLM推断不完整的项目上下文。

Result: 在真实工业项目复杂焦点方法和Defects4J基准上，CATGen相比现有LLM方法显著提高了编译成功率和结构覆盖率，同时大幅减少生成时间和token消耗。

Conclusion: 可靠的LLM单元测试生成实践更多依赖于基于真实开发约束的系统性工程支持，而非单纯的提示工程。

Abstract: Automated unit test generation has recently benefited from advances in large language models (LLMs), yet our industrial deployments reveal a persistent gap between promising research results and practical usability. In real-world projects with complex frameworks and cross-file dependencies, LLM-generated tests frequently fail to compile, require costly manual repair, or provide unstable coverage improvements. This paper reports our experience in designing, deploying, and evaluating CATGen, a context-aware workflow for LLM-based unit test generation, informed by repeated industrial failures and refinements. Rather than relying on LLMs to infer incomplete project context, we found that compilation robustness critically depends on making project-level dependencies explicit, stabilizing test class scaffolding, and replacing iterative LLM-based repair with lightweight static analysis. These experience-driven insights shaped CATGen's multi-stage design, which combines structured context retrieval, deterministic test skeleton construction, and program analysis-based post-processing. We evaluate CATGen on real-world complex focal methods from proprietary industrial projects and additionally on the Defects4J benchmark to assess generalizability. Across both settings, CATGen substantially improves compilation success and structural coverage while significantly reducing generation time and token consumption compared to existing LLM-based approaches. Our results demonstrate that reliable LLM-based unit test generation in practice depends less on prompt engineering alone and more on systematic engineering support grounded in real-world development constraints.

</details>


### [9] [Bridging Behavior and Implementation: Automated Java Glue Code Generation for Behavior-Driven Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19703)
*Xinyu Shi,Zhou Yang,An Ran Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoGlue是一个层次化多智能体框架，用于自动化Java粘合代码生成。它采用行为优先的工作流程，通过行为解释器理解步骤意图，开发者智能体检索相关BDD工件和项目代码后生成粘合代码。在8个开源项目的1,307个步骤上评估，相比少样本提示，API F1提升58.7%，CodeBLEU提升43.7%，46.1%的步骤生成了可直接使用的粘合代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDD中粘合代码的开发和维护需要同时理解预期行为和底层代码库，随着需求演变成为劳动密集型工作。虽然LLM在代码生成方面表现强大，但尚未被探索用于自动化粘合代码生成，该任务需要推理未充分指定的行为、相关BDD工件和大型项目代码库。

Method: 提出AutoGlue层次化多智能体框架，采用行为优先工作流程，将行为解释、上下文检索和代码生成分离。包含三个核心组件：1) Behavior Interpreter（行为解释器）从场景上下文推导步骤意图；2) Developer agent（开发者智能体）检索相关BDD工件和项目代码；3) 最终生成粘合代码。

Result: 在8个开源Java项目的1,307个步骤上评估，相比少样本提示，API F1提升58.7%，CodeBLEU提升43.7%。46.1%的步骤生成了可直接使用的粘合代码，大部分部分正确输出仅需微小修改。消融实验表明行为解释和项目感知的上下文检索对生成质量均有显著贡献。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效连接自然语言行为规范与项目代码，支持规范驱动的软件开发。

Abstract: Behavior-Driven Development (BDD) helps technical and non-technical stakeholders share a common understanding of software requirements through natural-language scenarios. Glue code makes these scenarios executable by mapping each step to the corresponding project code. However, developing and maintaining glue code requires knowledge of both the intended behavior and the underlying codebase, making it a labor-intensive part of BDD as requirements evolve. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong code generation capabilities, their use for automated glue code generation remains unexplored. This task requires reasoning over underspecified behavior, related BDD artifacts, and large project codebases. We present AutoGlue, a hierarchical multi-agent framework for automated Java glue code generation. AutoGlue follows a behavior-first workflow that separates behavior interpretation, context retrieval, and code generation. A Behavior Interpreter derives the intent of a step from its scenario context, while a Developer agent retrieves relevant BDD artifacts and project code before generating the final glue code. We evaluate AutoGlue on 1,307 steps from eight open-source Java projects. Compared with few-shot prompting, AutoGlue improves API F1 by 58.7% and CodeBLEU by 43.7%. It produces directly usable glue code for 46.1% of the evaluated steps, while most partially correct outputs require only minor revisions, such as adding missing actions or refining parameters. Ablation results show that behavior interpretation and project-aware context retrieval both contribute substantially to generation quality. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively connect natural-language behavior specifications with project code and support specification-driven software development.

</details>


### [10] [Towards Automated Formal Verification of zkEVMs Using LLM-Guided Constraint Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19795)
*Shichen Huang,Zhenghe Jiang,Yi Jiang,Ling-I Wu,Jingyang Li,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出VeriSynth框架，利用LLM作为形式化前端将Rust zkEVM代码翻译为Python/Z3符号约束，并通过SMT求解器验证正确性，在首个zkEVM源码级验证基准上达到90%以上的bug检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: zkEVM中实现bug（如gas计算错误）会导致有效证明认证语义错误的状态，从而无声地破坏密码学安全保证。现有形式化验证依赖不可持续的手动规约，LLM方法则存在幻觉且缺乏形式化保证。

Method: VeriSynth采用混合范式：LLM仅作为形式化前端将Rust代码翻译为符号约束，SMT求解器作为正确性仲裁器。集成语义分解、检索增强提示和验证引导的自动修复，形成闭环流水线，从Rust zkEVM代码合成可执行的Python/Z3验证模型。

Result: 在首个源码级zkEVM验证基准上，VeriSynth的bug检测率超过90%，显著优于直接LLM、对话式LLM基线以及生产级手工变异测试套件。消融实验证实每个流水线组件对整体效果都至关重要。

Conclusion: VeriSynth通过LLM与SMT求解器的混合范式，有效解决了zkEVM代码形式化验证的规约瓶颈问题，为保障zkEVM实现的密码学安全性提供了可行的自动化验证方案。

Abstract: Zero-Knowledge Ethereum Virtual Machines (zkEVMs) secure Ethereum rollups by generating zero-knowledge proofs that guarantee off-chain execution correctness. However, subtle implementation bugs (e.g., incorrect gas accounting) can lead to valid proofs certifying semantically faulty states, thereby silently defeating cryptographic guarantees. Formal verification via SMT solvers can prevent this, but is bottlenecked by specification: current zkEVM development practice lacks automated methods to translate Rust opcode handlers into verification models. Current practices rely on unsustainable manual specifications, while LLM-based approaches suffer from hallucination and lack formal guarantees. To address this, we propose VeriSynth, a framework that synthesizes executable Python/Z3 verification models from Rust zkEVM code. VeriSynth enforces a hybrid paradigm: an LLM acts strictly as a formalization frontend to translate code into symbolic constraints, while an SMT solver serves as the correctness arbiter. To handle complex multi-component state transitions, VeriSynth integrates semantic decomposition, retrieval-grounded prompting, and verification-guided auto-repair into a closed-loop pipeline. We evaluate VeriSynth on the first source-level zkEVM verification benchmark, encompassing both correct and faulty opcode implementations. VeriSynth achieves a bug detection rate of over 90%, substantially outperforming direct and conversational LLM baselines, as well as a production-grade handwritten mutation-testing suite. Ablation studies confirm that each pipeline component is critical to the framework's overall effectiveness.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Fail-to-Pass: Iterative Hardening of Co-Generated Bug Reproduction Tests and Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19843)
*Yuhao Tan,Zhibang Yang,Fangkai Yang,Yuan Yao,Yu Kang,Lu Wang,Pu Zhao,Xin Zhang,Xiaoxing Ma,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CoHarden框架，通过识别并利用"宽松"（Lax）的失败-通过（F->P）测试信号，在程序修复中迭代强化测试和补丁，以排除表面上通过但实际不正确的补丁，在SWE-bench Verified上达到69.4%的Resolved率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Bug复现测试（BRT）生成主要依赖F->P（在错误代码上失败、在正确修复上通过）作为评价标准，但该标准不足以确保BRT能有效提升下游程序修复效果。部分F->P测试是"宽松"的，虽然能复现观察到的症状，但仍会接受看似合理但不正确的补丁，导致修复成功率受限。

Method: 提出了CoHarden协同生成框架：1）首先生成测试，再生成修复；2）识别"宽松"（Lax）信号——即补丁通过F->P检查但实际错误的伪修复；3）利用Lax信号作为循环内收敛条件，迭代强化测试和补丁，直至生成的测试不再允许Lax回归。同时揭示了协同生成中存在的"测试-修复错误耦合"问题。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，CoHarden达到69.4% Resolved和78.9% F->P，相比最强的纯修复基线和协同生成基线，Resolved分别提升+9.6和+7.9个百分点，且在不同LLM骨干和基准上表现一致。

Conclusion: F->P标准不足以评估BRT对下游修复的有效性，需区分"严格"和"宽松"BRT；CoHarden通过迭代强化测试和补丁抵抗Lax回归，显著提升修复成功率，验证了该方向的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made automated program repair (APR) increasingly practical for real-world bugs, but repairing directly from bug reports remains underconstrained. Bug reproduction tests (BRTs) help close this gap by turning a bug report into an executable, bug-specific signal that can guide repair and validate candidate patches. Existing work has therefore studied BRT generation as a core subproblem in APR and mainly evaluates a generated BRT using the fail-to-pass (F->P) criterion, which requires the test to fail on the buggy code but pass on the golden fix. We show that F->P alone is insufficient when the goal of a BRT is to improve downstream repair. In particular, some F->P BRTs are lax, reproducing the observed symptom yet still admitting plausible-but-incorrect patches. We formalize this missing quality dimension by separating F->P BRTs into rigorous and lax ones, and show empirically that only the former consistently improve repair success. We further find that co-generation introduces test--fix error coupling, where the in-trajectory fail-to-pass (F->P) check can pass even when both the generated patch and generated test are wrong. Based on these findings, we propose CoHarden, a co-generation framework that uses the Lax signal as an in-loop convergence criterion. CoHarden first generates a test before any fix, then iteratively hardens the test and fix against surviving mutation patches until the generated test no longer admits Lax regressions. Experiments show that CoHarden reaches 69.4% Resolved and 78.9% F->P on SWE-bench Verified, outperforming the strongest fix-only and cogeneration baselines by +9.6 and +7.9 percentage points in Resolved, respectively, with consistent gains across LLM backbones and benchmarks.

</details>


### [12] [Towards Reliable C-to-Rust Translation with Rule-Guided Reasoning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19966)
*Feng Luo,Jiachen Liu,Cuiyun Gao,Jia Feng,Kui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAVEL框架，结合基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的Rust规则引导推理路径构建和强化学习（耦合执行反馈与推理质量信号），用于改进大语言模型在C到Rust自动翻译中的语法正确性和语义保真度。在xCodeEval、OS-Bench和HW-Bench三个数据集上，TRAVEL在计算精度、编译成功率和unsafe率等指标上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将遗留C程序迁移到Rust对提升内存安全性至关重要，但手动重写成本高昂。虽然利用LLM进行自动化C到Rust翻译前景广阔，但现有方法存在两大局限：LLM对Rust特有语法规则的识别能力不足，以及难以准确捕获复杂代码的语义，导致翻译结果经常出错。

Method: TRAVEL框架包含两个模块：(1) 基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的推理路径构建模块，以Rust特定规则引导搜索，使推理步骤遵循LLM常违反的语法规则；(2) 强化学习模块，耦合执行反馈与推理质量信号，鼓励模型构建能准确捕获程序语义的推理路径，确保生成的Rust代码保留原C程序的行为。

Result: 在xCodeEval上，TRAVEL相比最强基线IRENE，计算精度（CA）提升26.22%，编译成功率（CSR）提升18.77%。在HW-Bench和OS-Bench上，CSR分别提升18.28%和16.51%，unsafe率（UR）分别降低13.06%和13.08%。

Conclusion: TRAVEL通过规则引导的推理和强化学习有效解决了LLM在C到Rust翻译中的语法错误和语义偏差问题，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法，展示了结合结构化推理与学习机制提升代码翻译质量的潜力。

Abstract: The migration of legacy C programs to Rust has become an important direction for improving software memory safety while alleviating the high cost of manual rewriting. Leveraging large language models (LLMs) for automated C-to-Rust translation has emerged as a promising direction. However, existing LLM-based approaches remain limited. On the one hand, LLMs exhibit limited capability in identifying Rust-specific rules, and inadequate handling of Rust syntax often results in incorrect translations. On the other hand, existing LLMs often struggle to accurately capture the semantics of complex code, resulting in incorrect translations. To address these challenges, we propose a Translation fRAmework Via rule-guided reasoning and rEinforcement Learning, namely TRAVEL, consisting of two modules. The first module employs Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-based reasoning path construction guided by Rust-specific rules, steering the search toward translation steps that respect the syntactic rules that LLMs frequently violate. The second module introduces reinforcement learning that couples execution feedback with reasoning-quality signals, encouraging the model to construct reasoning paths that accurately capture program semantics, thereby ensuring that the generated Rust code preserves the intended behavior of the original C program. We evaluate TRAVEL on three datasets: xCodeEval (a public benchmark), OS-Bench (functions collected from the Linux kernel), and HW-Bench (an industrial dataset from Huawei). On xCodeEval, TRAVEL outperforms all baselines across three backbone LLMs. In particular, compared to the strongest prompting baseline IRENE, TRAVEL improves computational accuracy (CA) by 26.22% and compilation success rate (CSR) by 18.77%. On HW-Bench and OS-Bench, TRAVEL further improves CSR by 18.28% and 16.51%, respectively, while reducing unsafe rate (UR) by 13.06% and 13.08%, respectively.

</details>


### [13] [SequenceFI: Non-intrusive Temporal Fault Injection for Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20050)
*Yuzhen Tan,Jian Wang,Bing Li,Shaolin Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SequenceFI是一个非侵入式的时间敏感故障注入框架，通过观测消息级事件、传播时序证据并在满足时序守卫时触发故障，解决了微服务系统中时序依赖故障难以复现的问题。在Kubernetes上的实验表明，SequenceFI实现了100%的时序精度，平均一次尝试即可找到有效配置，端到端搜索时间减少95.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的请求级故障注入技术虽然能控制故障注入的位置和类型，但无法控制故障在分布式执行中被激活的时间点，导致时序依赖的故障（如状态变更后的失败、对并发响应的顺序敏感性、重复下游调用的部分失败）难以复现。

Method: SequenceFI采用非侵入式方法：1) 观测消息级收发事件；2) 沿请求执行传播紧凑的时序证据；3) 仅在满足时间敏感的时序守卫时触发故障；4) 从追踪数据中综合生成时序守卫，减少穷举搜索需求，无需修改应用代码或序列化库。

Result: 在Kubernetes上对4个广泛使用的微服务基准测试进行评估，涵盖9个时序故障场景和450次有效试验，SequenceFI实现了100%的时序成功率（无早发或多发），平均一次尝试找到有效配置，端到端搜索时间相比H-Random减少95.91%。

Conclusion: SequenceFI有效解决了微服务系统中时序依赖故障的复现问题，无需修改应用代码即可实现高精度的时序故障注入，大幅降低了配置搜索开销。

Abstract: Fault injection is widely used to evaluate the resilience of microservice systems, where client requests often span multiple services and execution stages. Existing request-level techniques usually control where and what faults are injected, but not when they are activated within a distributed execution. This limitation makes it difficult to reproduce timing-dependent failures, such as failures after state-changing side effects, order-sensitive concurrent responses, and partial failures among repeated downstream calls. This paper presents SequenceFI, a non-intrusive framework for temporal fault injection in microservice systems. SequenceFI observes message-level send and receive events, propagates compact temporal evidence along request executions, and triggers faults only when occurrence-sensitive temporal guards are satisfied. It further synthesizes temporal guards from traces, reducing the need for exhaustive enumeration of temporal fault-injection configurations, while requiring no modifications to application code or serialization libraries. We implement SequenceFI on Kubernetes and evaluate it on four widely used microservice benchmarks. Across nine temporal-fault scenarios and 450 valid trials, SequenceFI achieves 100.0\% temporal success without premature or multiple injections, finds effective configurations in one attempt on average, and reduces aggregate end-to-end search time by 95.91\% compared with H-Random.

</details>


### [14] [Multi-stage Dynamic Selection for Cross-Project Defect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20151)
*Juscimara G. Avelino,Juscelino S. A. Junior,George D. C. Cavalcanti,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段多分类器系统（MCS）选择框架，用于解决跨项目缺陷预测（CPDP）中训练与目标项目之间的分布偏移问题。第一阶段在项目级别评估多种MCS配置，选择覆盖多个训练项目且泛化能力强的配置；第二阶段在测试时按模块选择最合适的分类器进行预测。实验基于82个项目、4个基准数据集，结果表明该方法在多数场景下优于现有CPDP方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPDP方法受训练项目与目标项目之间分布偏移的影响，模型性能下降。

Method: 提出两阶段多分类器系统选择框架：第一阶段项目级选择，评估多种MCS配置以找到覆盖多训练项目且泛化好的配置；第二阶段模块级选择，在测试时为每个新模块动态选择最合适的分类器。

Result: 在82个项目、4个基准数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在多数场景下优于现有最先进CPDP方法。

Conclusion: 两阶段MCS选择框架通过模块级模型选择，增强了系统对训练与目标项目间分布变化的鲁棒性，有效提升了跨项目缺陷预测性能。

Abstract: Cross-Project Defect Prediction (CPDP) involves building models using data from external projects, called training projects, to predict modules from the target project. However, traditional CPDP methods suffer from the distribution shift between training and target projects that affects the model's performance. This paper proposes a novel CPDP framework that addresses this issue by proposing a two-stage multiple classifier system (MCS) selection scheme: one working at the project level and another at the module level. In the first stage, the framework evaluates multiple possible MCS configurations to find one that covers and generalizes well across multiple training projects. Consequently, the proposal is likely to obtain a diverse set of classifiers, each specialized in tackling software modules with distinct characteristics. The second selection stage operates at test time, selecting the most competent classifiers to predict each new module in the target project. Unlike previous approaches that apply the same classifiers to the entire target project, the proposed framework performs module-level model selection. This way, the system is more robust to changes in distributions between training and target projects because the selected set of classifiers is module-dependent. Our experimental results using 82 projects from four different CPDP benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms the state-of-the-art CPDP methods in most scenarios. The code, dataset, and further details about the proposed method are publicly available at https://github.com/jsaj/Multi_DES.

</details>


### [15] [How Developers Use Relation Chains in Gerrit-Based Review Ecosystems: An Empirical Study Across Three Open-Source Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20189)
*Ahmed Belhouchette,Moataz Chouchen,Marouene Chaieb,Mohammad Hamdaqa Abdelwahab Hamou-Lhadj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 开发者通过关联链（relation chains）在Gerrit中组织相关变更序列，这些链在15个仓库中的采用率从5%到49%不等，链式变更的合并时间比单变更长2.6倍，33.5%的链成员在审查过程中发生结构演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者越来越多地通过提交一系列相关变更而非单一变更来协调依赖性的审查工作流，这种依赖关系形成的关联链已成为Gerrit中的重要审查单元，影响了审查活动。

Method: 对来自OpenStack、Wikimedia和ONAP三个Gerrit生态系统的15个仓库中的29,580条关联链（共401,256个变更）进行分析，采用Mann-Kendall趋势检验、Mann-Whitney检验与Cliff's delta进行链式与单变更比较，以及Spearman相关分析基-后代依赖关系。

Result: 链式变更的采用率在14/15的项目中呈上升趋势，链式变更合并时间中位数是同等大小单变更的2.6倍，且差距随变更规模扩大而增大；基变更的审查活动与后代变更的审查活动共变（14-15个项目中的rho=0.43-0.61）；33.5%的链成员在审查期间发生结构演变。

Conclusion: 关联链作为持久且受生态系统塑造的协调单元运作，具有内部结构，传统以单个变更为中心的分析无法捕捉这些特征。未来的审查分析、审阅者分配系统和AI辅助审查工具应基于链而非孤立变更进行推理。

Abstract: Background. Developers increasingly coordinate dependent review workflows by submitting sequences of related changes rather than monolithic ones. In Gerrit, these dependencies form relation chains: structured review units that link changes together. As chains become more common, they shape review activities through synchronization overhead, CI amplification, and merge-ordering constraints.
  Aim. We investigate how developers adopt relation chains and how these dependency structures influence review dynamics and outcomes.
  Method. We analyze 29,580 relation chains from 15 repositories across three Gerrit ecosystems (OpenStack, Wikimedia, and ONAP), comprising 401,256 changes, using Mann-Kendall trend tests, Mann-Whitney tests with Cliff's delta for chain-vs-solo comparisons, and Spearman correlations for base-descendant dependencies.
  Results. Chain prevalence ranges from 5% to 49% across projects, increasing in 14 of 15. Chain changes take a median of 2.6 times longer to merge than size-matched solo changes, with the gap widening for very large changes. Review effort propagates through dependency-linked review workflows: base-change review activity co-varies with descendant review activity (rho = 0.43-0.61 in 14-15 of 15 projects), and 33.5% of chain members undergo structural evolution during review.
  Conclusions. Relation chains operate as durable, ecosystem-shaped coordination units with internal structure that change-centric analyses cannot capture. Future review analytics, reviewer-assignment systems, and AI-assisted review tools should reason over chains rather than isolated changes.

</details>


### [16] [Don't Trust the Label: License Laundering in AI Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20300)
*James Jewitt,Hao Li,Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过追踪232,270条数据集→模型→应用链，量化了AI供应链中许可证洗钱的两种形式：无许可证标注和许可证类别替换。结果显示62.3%的链条存在至少一个无许可证工件，所有义务性许可证的端到端存活率低于7%，而宽松类许可证达95.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI工件在跨平台供应链中携带许可证且其义务应通过再传播传递，但尚无研究衡量这些义务是否在供应链下游得以保留或被剥离替换。

Method: 追踪232,270条从Hugging Face数据集到模型再到GitHub应用的供应链链条，量化两种许可证洗钱形式：无许可证工件在下游获得标签，以及已声明许可证类别在再传播中被替换。

Result: 62.3%的链条经过至少一个无许可证工件（集中在少数基础数据集）；所有义务性许可证类别端到端存活率低于7%，而宽松类达95.1%。

Conclusion: 基于发现为从业者、模型发布者、权利持有者和平台所有者提供可操作的改进建议。

Abstract: AI artifacts move through a multi-platform supply chain, spanning datasets and models on Hugging Face and applications on GitHub. While each artifact carries a license whose obligations should propagate through redistribution, no study has yet measured whether those obligations survive the chain or are stripped and replaced as artifacts move downstream. We trace 232,270 dataset$\rightarrow$model$\rightarrow$application chains and quantify two forms of license laundering: when artifacts with no declared license acquire definitive labels downstream, and when one declared license category replaces another during redistribution. We find that 62.3% of chains pass through at least one artifact with no declared license (concentrated in a small set of foundational datasets), and that every obligation-bearing license category falls below 7% end-to-end survival while the Permissive category reaches 95.1%. Based on these findings, we provide actionable recommendations for practitioners, model publishers, rights holders, and platform owners.

</details>


### [17] [Multi-Source and Cross-Scenario Strategy-Guided Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20353)
*Yuwei Zhao,Qianyu Xiao,Ye Cui,Yijun Yu,Yingfei Xiong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MoST是一个基于LLM的代码优化框架，通过跨知识源和跨场景的策略整合，在多个编程语言的优化任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略引导的代码优化方法存在两个局限：(1)无法利用教科书、网页等其他知识源中的优化策略；(2)一个策略可能适用于不同场景（如不同编程语言），但现有方法只能将策略形式化到原始提交所属的场景。

Method: MoST将不同知识源中的项目统一表示为证据对象，通过跨源跨场景聚类识别策略，并在必要时跨场景迁移以生成静态分析规则。采用自平衡加权聚类算法平衡不同知识源的证据对象，以及示例迁移程序保证跨场景规则质量。

Result: 在151个C/C++、150个Python和50个Rust历史优化任务上，相比SemOpt，MoST生成的与开发者补丁完全相同或语义等价的补丁分别多出24.44%-180.00%和21.88%-37.50%。在15个真实项目优化中，性能测试最大提升19.72%-717.42%，平均提升4.44%-258.17%。

Conclusion: MoST通过整合多知识源和跨场景迁移策略，显著提升了LLM代码优化的效果，在多个编程语言和真实项目上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Automated code optimization improves program performance by refactoring source code, and recent studies use LLMs to generate optimization patches. The newest approaches are strategy-guided: they summarize strategies from historical optimization commits as static analysis rules, and use these rules to match code locations for LLMs to optimize. However, these approaches have two limitations: (1) the strategies may come from other knowledge sources, such as textbooks and web pages, but the existing approaches cannot utilize them; (2) a strategy may be applicable to different scenarios, e.g., different programming languages, but existing approaches can only formalize strategies for the scenario to which the source commit belongs. To address these limitations, we propose MoST, an LLM-based code optimization framework that integrates multiple knowledge sources across scenarios. MoST uniformly represents items in different knowledge sources as evidence objects, clusters them in a cross-source and cross-scenario manner to identify strategies, and transfers them to the target scenario when necessary for generating static analysis rules. To implement this process, MoST employs a novel self-balanced weighted clustering algorithm to balance evidence objects from different knowledge sources, and a novel example transfer procedure to ensure the quality of the generated rules when transferring across scenarios. On a benchmark containing 151 C/C++, 150 Python, and 50 Rust historical optimization tasks, compared with SemOpt, MoST yields 24.44%-180.00% and 21.88%-37.50% more patches that are exactly the same as or semantically equivalent to developer patches, respectively. When optimizing 15 real-world projects, MoST achieves 19.72%-717.42% maximum improvements and 4.44%-258.17% average improvements for the performance tests in the projects, significantly outperforming SemOpt and Codex.

</details>


### [18] [The ICSE 2026 Shadow PC: Training the Next Generation of Reviewers Through Deliberate Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20396)
*Christian Kästner,Michael Hilton,Jonathan Bell,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ICSE 2026 Shadow PC项目通过多阶段结构、校准与同行反馈机制、严格与主PC分离以及领导力发展路径，成功实现了大规模的审稿人培训，102名参与者完成项目并审稿117篇，97%推荐该体验，67%作者认为评审有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究中同行评审至关重要，但审稿人培训长期以来缺乏系统性，仍然停留在隐性学习层面。

Method: 重新设计的Shadow PC项目包含：多阶段结构（含校准与同行反馈）、与主PC严格分离、领导力发展路径。通过大规模实践验证其有效性。

Result: 102名参与者完成项目并审稿117篇；97%参与者推荐该体验；67%作者认为评审有帮助。

Conclusion: 该程序证明严格的审稿人培训可以在大规模下实现；提出Shadow PC区域主席机制以实现可持续扩展和领导力发展。

Abstract: Peer review is essential to software engineering research, yet reviewer training remains largely implicit. We describe the ICSE 2026 Shadow PC, a redesigned program emphasizing deliberate practice at scale. Key innovations include multi-phase structure with calibration and peer feedback, strict separation from the main PC, and a pathway toward leadership development. With 102 participants completing the program and reviewing 117 papers, 97% recommending the experience, and positive reception from authors (67% finding reviews helpful), the program demonstrates that rigorous reviewer training is achievable at scale. We share lessons learned and propose shadow PC area chairs as a mechanism for sustainable scaling and leadership development.

</details>

### [19] [FineServe: A Fine-Grained Dataset and Characterization of Global LLM Serving Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19349)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Shaoyuan Huang,Mingyuan Wang,Yunfeng Zhao,Xiaofei Wang,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FineServe 是一个从全球商业平台收集的多模型 LLM 服务真实工作负载数据集，支持细粒度分析异构模型和任务下的到达动态与 Token 行为，并提供了可配置的工作负载生成器，用于评估路由、调度和容量规划策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 服务负载研究多依赖代理轨迹或粗粒度表征，无法捕捉现代多模型平台的异构性，亟需真实、细粒度的多模型工作负载数据集。

Method: 从全球商业市场收集真实多模型工作负载数据，构建 FineServe 数据集；通过分析不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下的到达动态与 Token 行为，揭示波动模式；开发可组合细粒度模型感知工作负载的生成器。

Result: 揭示了不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下根本不同的波动模式；提供了评估路由、调度和容量规划策略的现实基础。

Conclusion: FineServe 为多模型 LLM 服务平台的基准测试提供了细粒度、真实的工作负载基础，有助于优化服务系统的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as always-on online services, making efficient LLM serving a critical systems challenge. Achieving low latency and high throughput under volatile demand requires deep understanding of real-world serving workloads, yet existing studies often rely on proxy traces or coarse-grained characterizations that fail to capture the heterogeneity of modern multi-model LLM platforms. We present FineServe, an in-the-wild, multi-model LLM serving workload dataset collected from a global commercial marketplace, enabling fine-grained characterization of real-world serving dynamics across heterogeneous models and tasks. Leveraging FineServe, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of arrival dynamics and token behavior, revealing fundamentally different fluctuation regimes across model architectures, scales and task intents. Building on these insights, we develop the FineServe workload generator, which composes fine-grained model-aware workloads into configurable mixtures tailored for benchmarking multi-model serving platforms. By exposing these fine-grained workload dynamics, FineServe provides a realistic foundation for evaluating routing, scheduling, and capacity-planning strategies in LLM serving systems. FineServe is available at https://github.com/hihiztc1/FineServe.

</details>


### [20] [Hybrid LSTM-Graph Neural Framework for Robust Financial Fraud Detection and Adversarial Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19350)
*Mariam Zakaria Moussa Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FraudShield AI，一种融合LSTM与图拓扑特征的混合框架，用于检测金融洗钱模式（如smurfing和layering），在PaySim数据集上显著优于Logistic Regression和XGBoost基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构在检测复杂洗钱模式（如smurfing和layering）时面临极端数据不平衡（欺诈率仅0.13%）和不断演化的对抗性规避策略，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出FraudShield AI混合框架，集成LSTM网络捕获时间序列特征，并结合手工设计的图拓扑特征（PageRank中心性、入度动态、自定义Flow Ratio）进行网络级取证分析；采用Focal Loss处理类别不平衡，设计动态阈值机制增强对低价值smurfing攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 在PaySim数据集上的实验表明，该混合模型在精确率、召回率和F1分数上显著优于Logistic Regression和XGBoost基线，尤其在难以检测的微交易欺诈模式上表现突出；消融研究证实了时间组件和拓扑组件的互补贡献。

Conclusion: FraudShield AI通过将交易分析从孤立视角转向网络级取证，有效提升了洗钱检测性能，为金融反欺诈提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Financial institutions face significant challenges in detecting sophisticated money laundering patterns, such as smurfing and layering, due to extreme data imbalance (0.13% fraud rate) and evolving adversarial evasion tactics. This paper proposes FraudShield AI, a hybrid framework that integrates Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks with hand-crafted Graph Topological Features to capture both temporal sequences and structural relational context. By engineering network-centric features including PageRank Centrality, In-Degree dynamics, and a custom Flow Ratio, the system shifts the detection paradigm from isolated transaction analysis to network-level forensics. A Focal Loss objective is used to address class imbalance, and a dynamic thresholding mechanism is introduced to improve resilience against low-value smurfing attacks. Experimental evaluation on the PaySim dataset shows that the proposed hybrid model substantially outperforms Logistic Regression and XGBoost baselines in Precision, Recall, and F1-Score, particularly on hard-to-detect micro-transaction fraud patterns. An ablation study confirms the complementary contribution of both the temporal and topological components.

</details>


### [21] [OpenEvoShield: Dual Non-Stationary Continual Defense for Open-World Multi-Agent System Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19351)
*Litian Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Yuting Zhang,Zejian Chen,Bingyu Yan,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenEvoShield是一个面向LLM多智能体系统的共演化持续防御框架，通过解耦攻防学习速率、动态行为边界、EWC正则化策略集成和能量基多粒度检测器，在100轮部署实验中有效检测未知攻击并保持低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在安全关键场景中面临敌手通过智能体间通信注入恶意指令的双重动态威胁：攻击策略持续演化，同时正常智能体行为随系统扩展发生漂移。现有防御将部署视为封闭世界问题，一旦分布偏移超出训练覆盖范围，性能迅速下降。

Method: 提出OpenEvoShield框架，包含四个模块：(M1)非对称速率控制器，从双重漂移信号中解耦攻击侧快学习速率和正常侧慢学习速率；(M2)正常边界更新器，以慢速率维护动态行为边界；(M3)EWC正则化策略集成，快速适应而不发生灾难性遗忘；(M4)能量基多粒度检测器，融合节点级、子图级和图级证据将新型攻击分类为分布外样本。

Result: 在5个基准和4种MAS拓扑上的100轮部署实验中，OpenEvoShield优于静态和持续基线方法，能够检测大多数前所未见的攻击，同时保持低误报率。

Conclusion: OpenEvoShield通过共演化持续防御框架有效应对LLM多智能体系统中的双重动态攻击威胁，验证了动态行为边界与多粒度检测结合在分布外攻击检测中的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where adversaries inject malicious instructions through inter-agent communication to propagate harmful behaviors. Unlike static threats, these attacks are doubly dynamic: adversaries refine injection strategies against deployed defenses while normal-agent behavior drifts with system expansion. Existing defenses treat deployment as a closed-world problem and degrade rapidly once either distribution shifts beyond training coverage. We propose OpenEvoShield, a co-evolutionary continual defense framework for LLM-MAS. An asymmetric rate controller (M1) decouples fast attack-side and slow normal-side learning rates from dual drift signals. A normal-boundary updater (M2) maintains a dynamic behavioral boundary at the slow rate, while an EWC-regularized policy ensemble (M3) fast-adapts without catastrophic forgetting. An energy-based multi-granularity detector (M4) fuses node-, subgraph-, and graph-level evidence to classify novel attacks as out-of-distribution. Experiments over 100 deployment rounds across five benchmarks and four MAS topologies show that OpenEvoShield outperforms static and continual baselines, detecting most previously unseen attacks while keeping false positive rates low.

</details>


### [22] [Benchmarking Confidential GPU Inference on NVIDIA H100 under Intel TDX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19353)
*Wei Wang,Abdul Hyee Waqas,Burns Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对NVIDIA H100 GPU在Intel TDX机密计算环境下的LLM推理性能进行了基准测试，发现机密模式会导致TTFT增加21.8%-27.8%，全局token吞吐量下降17.7%-21.1%，且大模型更早达到饱和拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算成为处理敏感输入或保护专有模型资产的AI推理工作负载的实际部署需求，但启用机密执行对GPU加速的大语言模型服务带来的性能代价尚不明确，需要进行系统性基准研究。

Method: 在单块NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU上，基于Intel TDX机密实例，对比标准非机密执行与机密计算模式。使用Mistral-7B v0.1和Qwen3-30B-A3B两种模型，测量首token时间、端到端延迟、每请求token生成吞吐量、全局token吞吐量和闭环请求吞吐量，并在递增并发度下进行实验。

Result: 固定请求率实验中，机密模式使Mistral-7B的平均TTFT增加21.8%，Qwen3-30B-A3B增加27.8%；全局token吞吐量分别下降17.7%和21.1%。闭环并发实验中，吞吐量差距维持在11.5%-20.2%范围，但大模型在机密模式下更早达到饱和拐点。

Conclusion: 机密GPU推理在负载下仍可保持可用吞吐量，但容量规划必须同时考虑稳态吞吐量损失以及大模型更早出现的饱和行为。

Abstract: Confidential computing is becoming a practical deployment requirement for AI inference workloads that process sensitive inputs or protect proprietary model assets. However, the performance cost of enabling confidential execution for GPU-accelerated large language model serving remains workload dependent and operationally important. This paper presents a benchmark study comparing standard non-confidential execution with confidential computing mode on a single NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU hosted in an Intel TDX confidential instance. The evaluation uses two representative language models, Mistral-7B v0.1 and Qwen3-30B-A3B, and measures time to first token, end-to-end request latency, per-request token generation throughput, global token throughput, and closed-loop request throughput under increasing concurrency. In fixed request-rate experiments, confidential mode increases average TTFT by 21.8% for Mistral-7B and 27.8% for Qwen3-30B-A3B, while global token throughput drops by 17.7% and 21.1%, respectively. In closed-loop concurrency experiments, throughput gaps remain in the 11.5-20.2% range, but the larger model reaches its saturation knee earlier under confidential mode. The results suggest that confidential GPU inference can retain usable throughput under load, but capacity planning must account for both the steady throughput penalty and the earlier saturation behavior observed for larger models.

</details>


### [23] [FormulaSPIN: Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Natural Language to Spreadsheet Formula Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19354)
*Cy Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FORMULASPIN是一个自博弈(self-play)框架，通过二元可执行性作为隐式监督信号，将Spreadsheet公式生成训练建模为双玩家博弈游戏，在不依赖额外数据的情况下突破监督微调的瓶颈，在NL2FORMULA基准上达到74.9%精确匹配和87.1%执行准确率，超越传统SFT和前沿闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格公式生成方法依赖静态监督数据，在小规模标注上快速饱和，而直接应用自博弈方法(如SPIN)会对执行等价但语法不同的公式产生矛盾的梯度信号，无法有效区分语义错误与合法风格变体。

Method: 提出FORMULASPIN框架：(1) 利用公式的二元可执行性作为隐式监督信号，区分语义错误与合法风格变体；(2) 将训练建模为双玩家博弈，主玩家学习偏好真实公式而非旧版本生成的公式，执行反馈将输出按粒度排序，实现从语义正确性到风格优化的自适应课程学习；(3) 引入ExecVote机制，通过语义级别的投票自然处理多种有效公式形式。

Result: 在多个基准上达到SOTA性能，NL2FORMULA任务上精确匹配74.9%，执行准确率87.1%，性能与使用额外偏好标注训练的模型相当，同时超越传统SFT和前沿专有模型。

Conclusion: 证明了自博弈在数据稀缺任务上的潜力，并为将自博弈扩展到可执行领域之外开辟了道路。

Abstract: Spreadsheet applications are used by hundreds of millions worldwide, yet writing formulas remains a significant barrier. Existing approaches rely on static supervised data, which quickly saturates on limited annotations. In this paper, we introduce FORMULASPIN, a self-play framework that breaks the ceiling of supervised fine-tuning by enabling iterative self-improvement without any additional data. Vanilla SPIN fails on this task: it uniformly penalizes every non-matching output, so execution-equivalent alternatives are punished as negatives in one example while serving as ground truth in another, producing contradictory gradients. Our framework resolves this by exploiting formula generation's unique advantage: binary executability provides implicit supervision that separates semantic errors from valid stylistic variants. We frame training as a two-player game in which the main player learns to prefer ground-truth formulas over those from its previous version, while execution feedback sorts outputs into distinct granularities-enabling an adaptive curriculum that shifts from semantic correctness to stylistic refinement. To further increase accuracy, we incorporate ExecVote, a semantic-level voting mechanism that naturally handles multiple valid formulations. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FORMULASPIN achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 74.9% exact match and 87.1% execution accuracy on NL2FORMULA, matching models trained with additional preference annotations while outperforming both traditional SFT and frontier proprietary models. These findings underscore self-play's potential to tackle scarce data tasks and open the door to extending it beyond executable domains.

</details>


### [24] [Information Discernment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19355)
*Joshua Ashkinaze,Laura Kurek,Alina Faisal,Tongyuan Miao,Mariam Joseph,Ceren Budak,Eric Gilbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信息辨别能力（信息辨别能力）概念，构建Learn2Discern（L2D）框架与基准，通过三个规范性公理评估LLM在整合外部知识时是否合理权衡信息来源可靠性（源辨别能力）和证据对先验信念的校正程度（真伪辨别能力）。实验覆盖13个模型、近67万次试验，发现模型在源辨别能力和真伪辨别能力上均接近随机水平，过度依赖来源流行度而非可靠性，且无论证据是否提升正确性都同等程度更新信念。新模型在真伪辨别能力上有提升，但源辨别能力停滞不前，简单推理时干预可改善两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地依赖外部知识源（如互联网），但缺乏评估模型是否合理权衡信息来源可靠性与证据真实性的系统框架与基准。现有研究未同时考察模型对信源质量的辨别能力（源辨别能力）和对证据校正先验信念程度的辨别能力（真伪辨别能力），而这对LLM作为信息整合工具的安全性与可信度至关重要。

Method: 形式化定义信息辨别能力，提出Learn2Discern（L2D）框架，基于三个规范性公理（源辨别能力、真伪辨别能力及其交互）构建可解释指标。通过预注册、配额匹配的用户研究（n=299）验证公理的外部有效性。在13个LLM上开展近67万次试验，评估模型表现，并测试简单推理时干预的效果。

Result: 用户研究证实所有三个公理得到用户认可，违反会降低信任与使用意愿。模型在源辨别能力和真伪辨别能力上表现接近随机，对来源流行度的依赖程度是可靠性的两倍，对改善或恶化信念的证据更新幅度几乎相同。模型在自身先验已较准确的数据集上整合外部知识最有效。更新更大的模型提升了真伪辨别能力但未改善源辨别能力。简单推理时干预可同时提升两种辨别能力。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在信息辨别能力上存在系统性缺陷，源辨别能力是模型规模无法弥补的盲点。随着LLM替代传统搜索引擎，这一对齐属性（信息辨别能力）的重要性日益凸显。简单推理时干预提供缓解方向，但根本性改进仍需更深入的研究。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used with external knowledge sources like the internet. Do they weigh information appropriately -- updating more for reliable sources (source discernment) and more when claims bring priors closer to the truth (truth discernment)? We formalize this as information discernment and introduce Learn2Discern (L2D), an experimental framework and benchmark grounded in three normative axioms with interpretable metrics. To establish external validity, a pre-registered, quota-matched user study (n=299) confirms that real LLM users endorse all three axioms and report that violations reduce their trust and usage intent. Across 13 models and nearly 670K trials, we find consistent failures across both dimensions: models perform near chance on source and truth discernment, rely on source popularity twice as much as source reliability, and update roughly equally whether a claim improves or worsens their position relative to the ground truth. Models integrate external knowledge most effectively on datasets where their priors are already the most accurate. Newer and larger models improve truth discernment but not source discernment, a blind spot that model complexity does not address. We identify simple inference-time interventions that improve both forms of discernment. We release our dataset and survey as a testbed for a core alignment property that scales in importance as LLMs replace traditional search.

</details>


### [25] [NEXUS: Structured Runtime Safety for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19356)
*Elias Hossain,Md Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Rajib Rana,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NEXUS是一个用于LLM智能体的结构化计划安全监控器，结合确定性规则、参数级检查和校准的逻辑回归风险评分，在四个干预动作（允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修订）中路由决策，在多个基准上优于纯规则方法，且延迟极低（0.205毫秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用工具的LLM智能体越来越多地执行高风险操作，运行时安全监控变得至关重要，现有方法缺乏细粒度的分级干预机制。

Method: NEXUS结合确定性安全规则、参数级检查和校准的逻辑回归风险评分，采用形式化的干预策略在允许、阻止、请求确认和请求修订四个动作中选择。

Result: 在128实例合成基准上F1=0.949，4类干预准确率0.6406（优于纯规则27.3个百分点）；R-Judge上F1=0.861 vs 0.849；AgentHarm上持平；IPI上0% ASR at 99%控制允许；NEXUS-Stress上F1=0.881；中位延迟0.205毫秒，开销低于0.1%。

Conclusion: NEXUS在低延迟下实现了有效的分级安全监控，但细粒度干预路由仍具挑战性，代码和基准已公开发布。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly execute high-impact actions, making runtime safety monitoring essential. We present NEXUS (Neural EXecution Utility and Safety), a structured-plan safety monitor that applies a formal intervention policy to select among four actions: allow, block, request confirmation, or request revision. NEXUS combines deterministic safety rules, argument-level inspection, and a calibrated logistic-regression risk score for graded escalation. On a 128-instance synthetic benchmark, NEXUS achieves an F1 score of 0.949 and a 4-class intervention accuracy of 0.6406, outperforming rule-only intervention selection by 27.3 percentage points. It also improves over rule-only on R-Judge (F1 = 0.861 vs. 0.849), matches rule-only on AgentHarm due to threat-model limits, and achieves 0% ASR at 99% control allow on IPI. On the rule-blind NEXUS-Stress benchmark, NEXUS reaches an F1 score of 0.881, highlighting the difficulty of fine-grained intervention routing. With 0.205 ms median latency, NEXUS adds under 0.1% overhead to typical agent loops. Code, benchmarks, and the calibrated risk scorer are publicly released.

</details>


### [26] [Stochastic Primal-Dual Decoding for Multiobjective Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19357)
*Dmitrii Moor,Ben Carterette,Senthilkumar Krishnamoorthy,Kyle Kretschman,Denis Beslic,Melissa Yalla,Alice Y Wang,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级推理时解码层，通过随机原始-对偶近似方案，在不修改或重新训练基础模型的情况下，为自回归生成式推荐系统实现多目标列表生成，理论保证约束违反和遗憾值，实验显示辅助目标提升+1.8%且用户满意度无损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐系统的多目标方法要么依赖为非生成式场景设计的后处理技术（未考虑生成式RS的序列特性），要么将辅助目标直接融入模型训练（大规模系统中不实用），因此需要一种轻量级、不修改模型且适配生成式序列特性的多目标推荐方法。

Method: 提出推理时解码层，将解码过程形式化为在线约束优化问题，基于剩余约束松弛量动态调整相关性与辅助目标之间的权衡，采用随机原始-对偶近似方案实现。

Result: 离线实验和大规模在线A/B实验显示，所提方法在多目标权衡上取得一致改进，辅助目标提升+1.8%且用户满意度无损失。

Conclusion: 所提轻量级推理时解码层能有效增强生成式推荐系统的多目标能力，无需重新训练模型，具备理论保证和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in recommender systems (RS) have shown substantial performance gains through generative modelling. In practice, recommendation often involves constructing slates -- ordered lists of items -- that must satisfy multiple objectives beyond relevance, such as constraints defined over item attributes or fairness constraints. Existing multiobjective approaches either rely on post-processing techniques designed for non-generative settings, or incorporate auxiliary objectives directly into model training. The former does not explicitly account for the sequential nature of generative RS, while the latter is often impractical in large-scale systems.
  We propose a lightweight, inference-time decoding layer that augments autoregressive generative RS to support multiobjective slate generation without modifying or retraining the underlying model. We formulate decoding as an online constrained optimisation problem, where items are selected sequentially, and trade-offs between relevance and auxiliary objectives are adjusted dynamically based on the remaining constraint slack, i.e., how much of each objective remains to be satisfied. This is implemented via a stochastic primal-dual approximation scheme that balances relevance and auxiliary objectives during generation.
  We provide theoretical guarantees on constraint violation and regret, and evaluate the proposed approach through extensive offline experiments and a large-scale online A/B experiment in a real-world recommender system. Our results show consistent improvements in multiobjective trade-offs, including a +1.8\% gain in the auxiliary objectives achieved at zero cost to user satisfaction.

</details>


### [27] [GraphContainer: A Unified Platform for Comparing and Debugging Graph RAG Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19362)
*Seonho An,Chaejeong Hyun,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphContainer是一个统一和可视化多样化图RAG工作流的平台，通过统一图表示层（UGR）标准化多格式图，并利用Graph Recorder追踪和可视化逐步检索过程，使研究人员能够便捷地比较不同图格式和检索策略，优化图RAG管道设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Graph RAG方法高度碎片化且不兼容，不同框架间图格式存在结构异构性，缺乏细粒度可视化工具，导致评估和比较检索行为极为困难。

Method: 提出GraphContainer平台，包含两个核心组件：(1) 统一图表示层（UGR），用于标准化多格式图；(2) Graph Recorder，用于追踪并以可视化方式呈现逐步检索过程。通过交互式Web界面实现异构图的导入和实时的可追踪视觉调试。

Result: 成功展示了GraphContainer导入异构图的能力，实现了图RAG方法的实时、可追踪的可视化调试，以及不同图格式和检索策略的受控比较。

Conclusion: GraphContainer降低了研究人员和实践者设计和优化图RAG管道的门槛，弥合了现有方法的碎片化差距。

Abstract: Graph RAG mitigates hallucinations and stale knowledge in LLMs, particularly for multi-hop question answering. However, existing approaches remain highly fragmented and incompatible. The structural heterogeneity of graph formats across different frameworks and the lack of granular visualization tools make it exceedingly difficult to evaluate and compare retrieval behaviors. To bridge this gap, we propose GraphContainer, a novel platform designed to unify and visualize diverse graph RAG workflows. GraphContainer features two key components: (1) a Unified Graph Representation (UGR) layer that seamlessly standardizes multi-format graphs, and (2) a Graph Recorder that tracks and visually renders the step-by-step retrieval process. Through an interactive web interface, we demonstrate GraphContainer's ability to import heterogeneous graphs and perform live, traceable visual debugging of graph RAG methods. Ultimately, we show how GraphContainer enables controlled comparisons of various graph formats and retrieval strategies, lowering the barrier for researchers and practitioners to design optimal graph RAG pipelines. A demonstration video is available at https://youtu.be/O02eNJLwkU0.

</details>


### [28] [LISA: Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention for Efficient Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19358)
*Yu Zhao,Zekun Zhang,Fan Jiang,Bo Zeng,Linlong Xu,Shimin Shan,Yu Liu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LISA提出了一种线性索引稀疏注意力机制，将推理复杂度从O(n²)降至O(nM)，在16K上下文下实现50%推理加速，并在推理基准上平均提升5.6%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1）在测试时扩展范式下推理上下文长度不断增加，但标准自注意力的O(n²)计算复杂度导致长序列推理成本急剧上升，限制了长COT推理在生产环境中的部署。

Method: LISA包含两个并行组件：(1) 线性注意力模块，提供O(n)时间复杂度的长程记忆；(2) Lightning索引器，从完整上下文中选择top-M重要token输入稀疏自注意力。两分支通过门控机制融合。采用两阶段训练：阶段1用知识蒸馏初始化线性注意力+滑动窗口注意力；阶段2引入索引器替代静态滑动窗口，通过逐头KL散度损失训练。

Result: 在DeepSeek蒸馏的Qwen模型上，LISA在16K token上下文下实现50%推理加速，在AIME和MATH-500等推理基准上平均性能提升5.6%。

Conclusion: LISA是一种即插即用的注意力替换模块，无需从头预训练，能有效降低长COT推理的计算成本并提升性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in long chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1 have led to increasingly longer inference context lengths under the test-time scaling paradigm. However, the O(n^2) computational complexity of standard self-attention causes inference costs to grow sharply with long sequences, limiting the deployment of long-CoT reasoning in production settings. To address this, we propose LISA (Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention), a plug-and-play attention replacement module that requires no pretraining from scratch. LISA integrates two lightweight components in parallel within the original model: (1) a Linear Attention module that provides long-range memory with O(n) time complexity; (2) a Lightning Indexer that selects the top-M important tokens from the full context to feed into a Sparse Self-Attention. The two branches are fused via a gating mechanism, reducing inference complexity from O(n^2) to O(nM) (M << n) for generating n tokens. We design a two-stage training pipeline: Stage 1 initializes the model by integrating the linear attention to capture long-range dependencies, complemented by a sliding-window attention mechanism that is optimized via knowledge distillation to approximate the full self-attention distribution of a frozen teacher model. In Stage 2, we further introduce the Indexer to replace the static sliding-window mechanism, enabling dynamic token selection from broader contexts. The Indexer is trained using a novel per-head KL divergence loss, which aligns its selection behavior with the attention patterns of the teacher model. Experiments on DeepSeek-distilled-Qwen models demonstrate that LISA achieves a 50% inference speedup under 16K-token context, while improving average performance by 5.6% on reasoning benchmarks including AIME and MATH-500.

</details>


### [29] [Profile-Graph Memory for LLM Agents: Implicit Cross-Entity Traversal through Narrative Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19359)
*Shengtong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多跳记忆基准MemHop（1,000道问题，跳数1-5，覆盖10个社交网络场景）和双层级记忆架构ProGraph（配置文件扩展+压缩残差），在MemHop上达到80.1%准确率，在LoCoMo上达78.4%，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体长期记忆基准主要评估单跳回忆，缺乏多跳关联评测能力。

Method: 提出MemHop基准（含逐跳证据标注）和ProGraph双层级记忆架构：(i) 配置文件扩展——基于子串匹配的实体名称遍历，无需显式知识图谱；(ii) 压缩残差——在每次配置文件更新时零额外API代价共提取精确日期、数量、命名项。

Result: 全网格消融实验显示机制专业化分工：配置文件扩展驱动多跳推理（移除后MemHop下降22.6pp），压缩残差驱动精确召回（未共提取时LoCoMo下降8.6pp），跨效应低于3pp。ProGraph在MemHop上平均80.1%（匹配FullContext参考线），LoCoMo上78.4%（超FullContext 11.3pp），优于Mem0、A-Mem、HippoRAG和RAG。

Conclusion: ProGraph通过轻量级双机制设计（无需显式知识图谱构建）同时在多跳推理和精确召回两项任务上达到最优，且机制间干扰极小。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that interact across sessions, yet current memory benchmarks primarily evaluate single-hop recall, leaving multi-hop association largely unmeasured. We make three contributions. First, we introduce MemHop, a multi-hop memory benchmark of 1,000 questions at hop depths 1-5 across 10 social-network scenarios, with per-hop evidence annotations. Second, we present Profile-Graph Memory (ProGraph), a two-layer memory architecture combining (i) profile expansion -- substring-matched traversal of entity names that naturally appear in LLM-written profile narratives, a minimal alternative to explicit knowledge-graph construction -- and (ii) compression residuals -- exact dates, quantities, and named items co-extracted with each profile update at zero extra API cost. Third, a full-grid ablation shows cross-benchmark mechanism specialization: profile expansion drives multi-hop reasoning (-22.6pp on MemHop when removed) while compression residuals drive precision recall (-8.6pp on LoCoMo when not co-extracted), with cross-effects under 3pp within a single architecture. ProGraph averages 80.1% on MemHop (matching the FullContext reference) and 78.4% on LoCoMo (exceeding FullContext by 11.3pp), outperforming Mem0, A-Mem, HippoRAG, and RAG on both. We release MemHop, ProGraph, and baseline implementations.

</details>


### [30] [Lifted Representation Hypothesis in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19360)
*Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"提升表征假设"：LLM通过共享潜在结构而非孤立事实来更新记忆。通过异常学习实验发现，LLM在嵌套规则与异常数据中易出现"碎片化"失败，而"提升"往往过早发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前尚不清楚LLM如何存储、选择和修正从个体观测中映射到一般规则的结构，作者希望系统性地研究这一过程。

Method: 提出"提升表征假设"，将"提升"定义为跨实例对称性的高效利用，"碎片化"定义为粗粒度提升结构细化成更具体子类型的过程。通过受控的异常学习实验，在上下文学习、LoRA和全参数微调三种范式下评估LLM的这两方面能力。

Result: LLM在数据受嵌套规则和异常支配时容易发生碎片化失败，而提升往往过早发生。

Conclusion: 需要进一步研究LLM中数据与规则结构之间的关系。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often answer queries by mapping individual observations to more general rule-like structures. However, it remains unclear how these structures are stored, selected, and revised. To study this process, we propose thelifted representation hypothesis: LLMs update memory through shared latent structures rather than isolated instance-level facts. This view frames lifting as an efficient use of symmetry across instances, and shattering as the refinement of coarse lifted structures into more specific subtypes. We evaluate LLMs' lifting and shattering through controlled exception-learning experiments across in-context learning, LoRA, and full fine-tuning. We find that LLMs are vulnerable to shattering failures when data are governed by nested rules and exceptions, while lifting often occurs prematurely. These results highlight the need to study the relation between data and rule structures in LLMs.

</details>


### [31] [AdaRoPE: Not All Attention Heads Should Rotate and Scale Equally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19363)
*Shaowen Wang,Yuke Zheng,Tansheng Zhu,Shuang Chen,Shaofan Liu,Suncong Zheng,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaRoPE，为每个注意力头分配可学习的旋转频率和注意力缩放因子，替代传统RoPE的统一频率调度，实验证明该方法在长上下文场景中优于现有RoPE变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RoPE对所有注意力头强制使用统一的频率调度和缩放，但不同功能角色的头需要不同的频率范围和注意力缩放因子，这种统一策略导致嵌入维度利用不充分，尤其在长上下文场景下性能下降。

Method: AdaRoPE为每个注意力头配备可学习的旋转频率和注意力缩放因子，通过头级别的优化来替代传统RoPE中统一的频率调度和缩放策略。

Result: 预训练LLM使用AdaRoPE一致优于现有RoPE变体（包括部分RoPE和NoPE基线）；在上下文扩展中，头特定缩放比统一缩放（如YaRN）更优，既能更好扩展上下文长度，又能保持短上下文性能。

Conclusion: 优化旋转位置嵌入应当细化到每个注意力头的级别，头特定优化对于提升长上下文性能至关重要。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) is widely adopted in Transformers to encode positional information, yet standard implementations enforce a uniform frequency schedule and scaling across all attention heads. Using simplified retrieval tasks and length generalization scenarios, we show -- both empirically and theoretically -- that heads with different functional roles require distinct frequency ranges and attention scaling factors to operate effectively. Ignoring this structure leads to suboptimal utilization of embedding dimensions and degraded performance, particularly under long-context settings. To address these limitations, we propose AdaRoPE, which equips each attention head with learnable rotation frequencies and attention scaling factors. Pretrained LLMs with AdaRoPE consistently outperform existing RoPE variants, including partial RoPE and NoPE baselines. For context extension, we further show that uniform frequency and attention scaling, used in methods such as YaRN, are suboptimal. By applying head-specific scaling, AdaRoPE enables better context extension while better preserving short-context performance in both the extrapolation setting and the long-context continued pretraining setting. These results highlight the importance of optimizing rotary position embedding at the level of individual attention heads.

</details>


### [32] [Statistically Grounded Sparse-Feature Interventions for Activation-Space Control in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19364)
*Oshayer Siddique,J. M Areeb Uzair Alam,Md Jobayer Rahman Rafy,Syed Rifat Raiyan,Hasan Mahmud,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种透明且无需优化的SAE特征引导（steering）流程，通过六条件可靠性过滤和Borda共识排序选择稀疏特征，并以Cohen's d加权组合SAE解码器行构建引导方向。实验在三个Gemma模型、四个行为领域和356种层-强度配置下进行，发现引导效果高度依赖于模型、领域、层和强度，且原始属性移动与质量保持生成之间存在显著差距。最强的逻辑正确性引导在Gemma 2 9B上达到+1.16的主评分差值，但研究更强调应报告质量条件下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SAE的激活引导方法通常依赖学习得到的引导目标或单一标准的特征选择，缺乏透明性和可解释性。本文旨在提出一种更透明、无需优化的特征选择与引导方向构建方法。

Method: 提出透明SAE特征引导流程：(1) 六条件可靠性过滤；(2) 基于F检验、KSG互信息和Cohen's d三个互补统计量的无权重Borda共识排序；(3) 用Cohen's d加权组合SAE解码器行构建引导方向，该方向在SAE特征近似解相关的假设下受Fisher-LDA启发，无需优化。

Result: 在三种Gemma模型、四个行为领域、356种层-强度配置下，该方法产生可测量的领域特定偏移。最强配置下逻辑正确性引导在Gemma 2 9B上主评分差值达到+1.16。但更重要的发现是，可用的引导高度集中在特定的模型、领域、层和强度组合上，且原始属性移动与质量保持生成之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 激活引导评估应同时报告质量条件下的成功率与原始行为偏移，而不仅仅是原始偏移量。引导效果高度局部化，提示实际应用中需要精细调参。

Abstract: Activation steering offers a lightweight alternative to fine-tuning for behavioral control of large language models, but SAE-based steering methods often rely on learned steering objectives or single-criterion feature selection. We introduce a transparent SAE-feature steering pipeline that first applies a six-condition reliability filter, then ranks sparse features through an unweighted Borda consensus over three complementary statistics: $F$-test, KSG mutual information, and Cohen's $d$. The resulting steering direction is constructed as a Cohen's-$d$-weighted combination of SAE decoder rows, providing an optimization-free direction motivated by Fisher-LDA under approximate SAE-feature decorrelation. Across three Gemma-family models, four behavioral domains, and 356 layer-strength configurations, the method produces measurable domain-specific shifts while revealing a substantial gap between raw attribute movement and quality-preserving generation. In the strongest configuration, logical-correctness steering reaches a primary-score delta of $+1.16$ in Gemma~2 9B; however, our broader finding is that usable steering is highly localized by model, domain, layer, and strength. These results argue that activation-steering evaluations should report quality-conditioned success alongside raw behavioral shift. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Oshayer-Siddique/LLM-Steering-Using-SAE.

</details>

*Mario Alviano,Lorenzo Grillo,Nicola Leone,Fabrizio Lo Scudo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种逻辑引导的数据抽取框架，将大语言模型与回答集编程（ASP）结合，通过ASP进行验证、推理、一致性检查和查询控制，在保证抽取质量的同时减少LLM调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在从非结构化文本中抽取语义数据时，对需要复杂组合推理和全局一致性的任务不可靠，现有流水线对所有目标谓词独立查询LLM，效率低且缺乏逻辑约束。

Method: 将LLM抽取与ASP推理交错执行：ASP识别逻辑上可接受的谓词并引导抽取查询，LLM仅查询这些谓词；ASP推导逻辑蕴含事实，减少LLM调用；引入缓存机制利用合取查询的单调性减少求解器调用。

Result: 在ASP推导的基准测试中，该框架减少了LLM调用次数，并通过抑制虚假输出提升了抽取质量。

Conclusion: 非单调逻辑编程（ASP）对受控语义提取具有重要价值，该框架在温和假设下与基线方法等价，但效率更高、质量更好。

Abstract: When Large Language Models (LLMs) are used for semantic data extraction from unstructured text, producing candidate relational facts from natural language, they may remain unreliable for tasks requiring complex combinatorial reasoning and global consistency. This paper proposes a logic-guided data extraction framework combining LLM-based extraction with Answer Set Programming (ASP). The LLM produces candidate facts, whereas ASP performs validation, inference, consistency checking, and control. Unlike existing pipelines that query the LLM independently for all target predicates, the proposed approach uses ASP reasoning to identify which predicates are logically admissible at each stage and to guide extraction queries. By interleaving LLM calls with ASP derivation, the framework infers logically implied facts without further extraction and detects inconsistencies early. We formalize the pipeline and prove that, under mild assumptions, it is equivalent to the baseline approach with respect to the final extracted facts, while requiring fewer LLM calls. We also introduce a caching mechanism for logic-based control queries, exploiting monotonicity of conjunctive queries over incrementally constructed fact sets to reduce solver invocations. Experiments on ASP-derived benchmarks show that the framework reduces LLM calls and improves extraction quality by mitigating spurious outputs, demonstrating the value of non-monotonic logic programming for controlled semantic extraction.

</details>

*Fumiaki Uehara,Koo Imai,Masato Tsutsumi,Keigo Kansa,Sora Usui,Yuki Kobiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征空间中，安全多面体(SaP)方法的约束数量K可收敛至2（12/14类别），并引入自适应锥约束替代固定数量平面约束，实现96-99%的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
